# `kernel_opt` domain (Plan 001 Phase 3, absorbs Proposal 012)

> The third `HealDomain` impl. Heals GPU kernel optimization. Absorbs
> Proposal 012's design (thermal-tier + latent-MoE + numeric validator) into
> the multi-domain framework. **4090 LoRA deferred** across all tiers above
> Plasma.

## Corpus

Batch 1 (Research 002): 21 rules — 2 original + 19 popcorn-distilled across 5 categories.
Batch 2 (Research 003): 7 rules — flash-attention tiling patterns.
Batch 3 (Research 004): 6 rules — linear-attention recurrence patterns.
Batch 4 (Research 005): 9 rules — MoE dispatch + backward-pass patterns.
Batch 5 (Research 006): 4 rules — NSA block-sparse selection patterns.
Batch 6 (Research 007): 3 rules — varlen packing + chunked reductions.
Batch 7 (Research 008): 9 rules — burn-cubecl autotune dispatch + conv-as-GEMM + memory layout.
Batch 8 (Research 009): 9 rules — burn-cubecl kernel polymorphism + dtype/alignment specialization + cubecl-opt IR strength reduction.
Batch 9 (Research 010): 8 rules — popcorn fusion + dtype/quant + dispatch (add_rms_norm, group_norm_linear, jsd, int8_int2_matmul, _quant).
Batch 10 (Research 011): 12 rules — popcorn compute + chunked reduction + polymorphism (mean_pooling, solve_tril, hadamard_transform, comba, dyt, token_shift, sparsemax, selective_log_softmax, softmax, rms_norm, geglu, swiglu, rope, gelu).
Batch 11 (Research 012): 11 rules — popcorn linear-attention recurrence family (gla, simple_gla, rwkv7, delta_rule, iplr_delta_rule, gated_oja_rule) + parallelism (chunk_global_cumsum, retention, lightning_attn) + flash-attn tiling (deltaformer) + compute (based, rebased, linear_attn) + dispatch (lightning_attn). A 12th candidate (chunk-reset-scan) was dropped as a duplicate of chunk-reset-cumsum (Batch 6).
Batch 12 (Research 013): 14 rules — popcorn gated delta family (gated_delta_rule, gated_delta_product) + specialized recurrences (mesa_net, mesa_net_decode, titans_linear, ttt, rwkv4, rwkv6) + advanced attention (wall_attn, multi_token_attention) + MoE gate (momoe) + KDA gate (kda_gate, kda_gate_cumsum) + hierarchical mask (log_linear_attn). Three candidates (kda per-key-dim gate, forgetting_attn cumsum decay, abc two-state softmax) were dropped as duplicates.
Batch 13 (Research 014): 6 rules — popcorn final unmined pass. RL loss stability (grpo_offpolicy: expm1 for KL cancellation) + complex-free RoPE (llama4_rope: paired reals) + multimodal RoPE (qwen2vl_mrope: cyclic modality table) + RWKV-7 channel mixing (rwkv7_channel_mixing: stateful cat-prefix shift + squared ReLU from sqrelu) + RWKV-7 gate output (rwkv7_gate_output: fused reduce-then-broadcast). rwkv7_k_update + sigmoid + tvd yielded no new rule.
Batch 14 (Research 015): 4 rules — **first batch mining riir-gpu (the stack's own production kernels), not external libraries**. Norm-fold into matmul (gemv_q4k_batched_rmsnorm: fold inv_rms scalar into the GEMV dot) + reduction-split pre-pass (compute_inv_rms: scalar pre-pass + fold) + single-plane reduction (Issue 611 fix: one plane suffices vs N redundant planes) + CPU-bottleneck offload (gpu_transpose Issue 402: GPU compute kernel writes directly into pooled memory). Two riir-gpu files (lora_fused SGMV, rope_geglu section-view) assessed + dropped (SGMV overlaps MoE grouped; section-view is pointer arithmetic not an opt).
Batch 15 (Research 016): 4 rules — **second batch mining riir-gpu**. Block-contiguous weight layout (gemm_ternary_block_contiguous: pack 3 separate weight buffers into 1 to cut global-memory accesses 3×) + fused dual GEMV + GeGLU (gemv_geglu_f16: fuse 2 sibling GEMVs + act into 1 dispatch, saves 52 dispatches/decode at 26 layers) + orthogonal pre-rotation (spectralquant/attention.rs: pre-rotate query once vs each key, ~4096× fewer rotation FLOPs) + water-fill tail-dim shared codebook (spectralquant/dequant.rs: top dims per-dim codebooks, tail dims share one low-bit codebook, 2.3× codebook memory reduction). One riir-gpu file (forward_flashprefill) assessed + dropped (cascade structure overlaps NSA Category I; 4 staged dispatches is a non-opt).
Batch 16 (Research 017): 4 rules — **third batch mining riir-gpu**. cmma hardware unit over scalar ALU (gemm_ternary_simdgroup: use the cooperative matrix multiply-accumulate hardware unit instead of scalar ALU — 64 FMAs/cycle on the dedicated unit) + dequant-once-per-K-tile into shared memory (gemm_ternary_simdgroup: dequant a weight tile once, let the hardware matrix unit consume it across all output rows — m× amortization) + swap-large-axis-to-X (gemm_ternary_simdgroup: put the large GEMM axis on the X cube-count for workgroup distribution) + norm-decoupled low-bit scoring (forward_turboquant: store per-position L2 norm separately, quantize the unit-normalized key at 2-4 bits, reconstruct score as norm × dot). Four files assessed + dropped (lora_fused SGMV overlaps Category H; spectralquant/rotation boilerplate; spectralquant/qjl composition of water-fill with JL; spectralquant/calibration two-pass reduce overlaps split-reduction).
Batch 17 (Research 018): 4 rules — **fourth batch mining riir-gpu (ternary scalar-ALU hot loop)**. Skip-all-zero-packed-words-via-or-reduce (gemm_ternary_batched: OR-reduce all tile rows' pos+neg bit-words, skip the 32-iteration sign-extraction loop when the whole tile's word is zero — ternary sparsity exploit) + hoist-group-invariant-scale-out-of-word-loop (gemv_ternary_block_contiguous: cache the per-group quant scale in a register across the 4 words it covers, reload only at the group boundary — 4× fewer global loads) + split-sign-accumulators-defer-subtraction (both ternary files: keep separate pos/neg accumulators inside the bit loop, subtract once at the end — 32→1 subtracts/word + independent ILP chains) + clamp-tile-indices-to-sentinel-discard-at-write (gemm_ternary_batched: alias OOB tile rows to m-1, compute fully, discard at write time — keeps the loop body uniform without padding overhead). Two files assessed + dropped (rope_geglu_cubecl: pairing-generic + precomputed cos/sin table are composition of existing dispatch/precompute principles; forward_dflare: 17-binding layout + heterogeneous KV are architectural, not kernel optimization).
Batch 18 (Research 019): 3 rules — **fifth batch mining riir-gpu (Q4_K quantized GEMV family)**. Row-tile-amortize-input-load (gemv_q4k_plane_rowtiled: assign N output rows per plane, load input once, reuse across N dot products — GEMV to small-GEMM via M-widening) + factor-affine-quant-scale-out-of-element-loop (gemv_q4k_plane_factored: when dequant is affine in the code within a sub-block, factor d·scale and dmin·min out — Q4_K-specific, ternary has no min offset to factor) + pin-lane-to-quant-sub-block-coalesce-byte-loads (gemv_q4k_plane_factored: remap lane→element so each lane is pinned to one sub-block — 8 loads→2, enables affine factoring). Three sibling Q4_K files (qkv, geglu, batched) assessed + dropped: qkv = composition of fuse-gate-up-gemv-activation (triple fusion); geglu = same fuse + Q4_K dequant; batched = no new opt.
Batch 19 (Research 020): 2 rules — **sixth batch mining riir-gpu (fused-dispatch family)**. Chain-dispatches-single-sync-point (ternary_ffn_fused: queue N dependent dispatches in one command buffer, sync only once at the end — command-buffer-level fusion distinct from single-kernel fusion; linear dependency chains keep intermediates bounded, fan-out is the known-bad case per Bench 437) + fuse-residual-via-in-place-output-array (gemv_ternary_residual_cubecl: when a kernel needs N+1 array params but the runtime limits to N, alias the output buffer as the accumulation input (read-then-write) — bypasses CubeCL v0.10's hard 5-array limit, collapses 128 dispatches/token → 64). Three sibling files assessed + dropped: deltanet_input_proj_fused (same pattern as ternary_ffn_fused — confirms generality); deltanet_chunked + base recurrence (covered by recurrent-vs-chunk-dispatch Batch 11).
Batch 20 (Research 021): 5 rules — **seventh batch mining riir-gpu (CUDA hardware-instruction cluster + CubeCL LUT + base-3 trit)**. Cooperative-block-wide-quantize-staging (gemv_ternary_cuda_raw `_fused` variant: ALL 256 threads cooperate to quantize f32→int8 into shared memory once, then each warp reads from shared — eliminates a separate staging dispatch + global round-trip; distinct from dequant-once-per-k-tile which is per-tile WEIGHT dequant, this is block-wide ACTIVATION quantize) + byte-perm-lut-via-permutation-immediate (gemv_ternary_cuda_raw: pack {−1,0,1,2} in one int, __byte_perm(constant, constant, code >> shift) decodes 4 codes to 4 signed bytes in ONE PTX instruction — first CUDA-specific HW-instruction rule, no CubeCL analogue) + dp4a-int8-hardware-dot-product (gemv_ternary_cuda_raw: NVIDIA __dp4a does 4-lane int8 dot in one instruction — distinct from cmma Batch 16 which is CubeCL 8×8×8 matrix tile op, this is scalar 4-lane int8 dot, CUDA-only) + threadgroup-subset-sum-lut-keyed-by-activation (gemv_ternary_cubecl LUT path gated `ternary_lut_gemv`: precompute 16-entry subset-sum table per 4-activation group in threadgroup memory, 4-weight ternary dot = 2 reads + 1 subtract; one build serves every row because the table is keyed by ACTIVATION subset (contains no weight/scale/zero-point) — structurally different amortization from the Q4_K LUT negative result which was keyed by weight+scale+zero-point and rebuilt 160 tables/row) + base-3-trit-packing-5-per-byte (gemv_ternary_cubecl trit path: pack 5 ternary per byte using base-3 mixed-radix, 17.6% denser than 2-bit codes; REGIME-DEPENDENT — wins on Metal, loses 2.1× on CUDA scalar ALU per Issue 607). Three Metal files assessed + dropped: all 3 are REFUTED end-to-end (3.72× kernel gain lost to 0.87×/0.95× integration overhead); 2 genuinely novel patterns (cross-runtime zero-copy handle extraction + pool-buffer aliasing hazard workaround) DEFERRED to Batch 21 for coherent treatment.
Batch 21 (Research 022): 5 rules — **eighth batch mining riir-gpu (Metal cross-runtime zero-copy integration cluster)**. Cross-runtime-raw-handle-extraction (gemm_ternary_metal_zero_copy: extract `id<MTLBuffer>` from CubeCL/wgpu handles via `as_hal::<Metal>().raw_handle()` to bypass wgpu-core validation — generic, applies to any wrapper-runtime→raw-native-dispatch integration without a host round-trip) + pool-buffer-aliasing-dedicated-output (when the runtime sub-allocates handles within one pool buffer, input/output aliasing breaks hazard tracking — fix: dedicated output buffer keyed by byte size + GPU→GPU copy back; distinct from inplace-output-alias Batch 19 which is parameter-count bypass) + same-queue-implicit-ordering-no-sync (dispatch on the shared command queue the wrapper uses — in-order execution makes explicit sync unnecessary; eliminates the host-round-trip serialization that refuted Issue 656) + per-dispatch-pointer-re-extraction (when the runtime can reallocate its pool between dispatches, raw pointers must be re-extracted on EVERY dispatch, not cached — the correctness invariant that makes raw-handle-extraction safe) + no-blocking-sync-in-async-pipeline (`wait_until_completed` serializes the async pipeline — use queue ordering + caller's pre-dispatch sync to cover correctness; complement of chain-dispatches-single-sync-point Batch 19). The 2 refuted siblings (`gemm_ternary_metal_tensor.rs` + `_wgpu.rs`) are covered by these general rules: the host-round-trip is the anti-pattern rule #3 fixes, the per-dispatch staging copies are a special case of rule #2 when the runtime can't extract raw handles.
Batch 22 (Research 023): 5 rules — **ninth batch mining riir-gpu (cudarc_kernels CUDA cluster — full GPU-resident forward port for Issue 615)**. Dynamic-shared-memory-runtime-sized (`extern __shared__` with launch-time `shared_mem_bytes` for runtime-sized dimensions like head_dim; fixes the Bonsai-27B `head_dim=256` bug where a compile-time `Shared::<[f32]>::new_slice(128)` was too small; cudarc_kernels/attention.rs + deltanet.rs) + ternary-bit-plane-u64-as-2x-u32 (two bit-planes packed as u64 blocks cast to 2×u32 for per-bit sign extraction; distinct from base-3-trit-packing-5-per-byte which is arithmetic base-3; cudarc_kernels/embedding.rs) + warp-shuffle-intrinsics-for-sub-warp-reduction (`__shfl_down_sync` butterfly reduction for blocks ≤32 threads = one warp; no smem alloc, no syncthreads; includes the `volatile float* vsmem` companion for the final 32-thread tail of a larger smem tree; cudarc_kernels/mod.rs + attention.rs) + fused-activation-quantize-for-gemv-consumer (fuse RMSNorm/SwiGLU/gate*sigmoid + absmax + int8 quantize into one kernel when the activation feeds a GEMV that reads int8; eliminates the intermediate f32 HBM round-trip; distinct from fuse-quant-matmul which is quantize+matmul; cudarc_kernels/mod.rs Issues 623/625/626/627) + cuda-graph-scalar-via-device-pointer (ship a `_devpos` variant that reads scalars from a device pointer dereferenced at kernel runtime so the captured CUDA graph can process different inputs each launch; scalar args get baked into the captured graph at capture time; cudarc_kernels/embedding.rs + attention.rs, Issue 618).
Batch 23 (Research 024): 5 rules — **tenth batch mining riir-gpu (CubeCL flash-attention decode cluster)**. Combined-kv-buffer-keys-then-values (store keys and values as a single contiguous buffer `[keys | values]` to halve buffer bindings + coalesce via one kv_off index; attention_cubecl/mod.rs L107-140) + gqa-kv-head-sharing-bandwidth-reduction (Grouped-Query Attention: share KV heads across query heads via `kv_group = head_idx * n_kv_head / n_head`; reduces KV cache bandwidth by n_head/n_kv_head×; e.g. 2× for Gemma 2 2B's 8:4 ratio) + softcap-tanh-attention-score (Gemma's `softcap * tanh(score/softcap)` bounds raw logits to [-softcap, +softcap]; smooth + differentiable unlike clamp; `softcap=0` disables for LLaMA/MiniCPM) + fold-query-tokens-for-occupancy (when `n_head < SM_count`, fold multiple speculative-decode query tokens into one workgroup via `fold_factor()` — largest divisor of `seq_len_q` ≤ `workgroup_size/n_head`; Plan 179 D1) + hardcoded-fast-path-jit-specialization (dispatch a hardcoded kernel variant when params match a known config so the JIT constant-folds dims into optimized Metal/WGSL shaders; ~24% decode throughput gain 31.4→41.4 tok/s on M3 Max; distinct from autotune which searches configs).
Batch 24 (Research 025): 4 rules — **eleventh batch mining riir-gpu (cudarc_kernels backward cluster — closes the cudarc_kernels/ directory, 6/6 files)**. Widen-index-multiply-to-64bit (cast ONE operand of an address-producing multiply to 64-bit when position × stride can exceed 2^31; int32 wraps silently in CUDA → OOB reads with no error; `(long)t * kv_stride` at every KV access; cudarc_kernels/backward.rs L416/L474/L478/L515/L518) + dual-accumulator-single-reduction-sweep (two block reductions over the SAME elements fused into one sweep: x loaded once feeding both partials, both smem arrays reduced inside the SAME stride tree — halves loads + halves the `__syncthreads()` count; RMSNorm backward's sum(x²) + dot(grad_y·γ·x) is the canonical case; backward.rs L61-83) + dual-grad-same-pass (both partials of a product-rule activation emitted from ONE kernel sharing input reads + one sigmoid evaluation — SwiGLU/z-gating/output-gate/beta-decay backward; backward.rs L108-126/L137-155/L169-186/L345-369) + broadcast-uniform-skip-reduce (thread-uniform values derived from already-reduced broadcasts must be broadcast from thread 0, not re-reduced — re-reducing sums the identical value blockDim.x times, a correctness bug; the documented T7.2 validation catch in softmax backward where z came out 256× too large; backward.rs L492-501). Five candidates dropped as duplicates: recompute/save-forward-state (= `activation-recompute-budget` + `precompute-scalar-reduction` + `split-reduction-scalar-prepass`), atomic scatter (= `atomic-scatter-reduction`), launch-loop→grid (= `multi-dim-grid-launch`), compute-then-scatter split (caller-simplicity, not perf), smem multi-array packing (= `dynamic-shared-memory-runtime-sized` context).
Batch 25 (Research 026): 5 rules — **twelfth batch mining riir-gpu (the Gemma cluster — gemma2_cubecl/ + gemma4_cubecl/)**. Device-side-argmax-minimal-download (greedy decode needs ONE u32, not the logits: run the argmax reduction on device, download 4 B instead of vocab_size×4 ≈ 1 MB/token — ~250,000× smaller boundary crossing, ~550 MB → ~50 MB over a 50+500 session; gemma2_cubecl/gpu_forward.rs L319-346 + L348-423) + monotone-transform-elision-under-argmax (softcap `cap·tanh(x/cap)` is strictly increasing → argmax unchanged → skip the 256K-tanh pass for greedy decode; the value-consuming CPU path still applies it; gpu_forward.rs L208-217; distinct from softcap-tanh-attention-score which APPLIES softcap in-kernel) + fan-out-shared-input-single-sync (N INDEPENDENT sibling kernels sharing one input — Q/K/V GEMVs: upload once, clone the handle, queue all, first read executes the batched submission — 1 upload + 1 sync instead of N + N; NOT the Bench 437 known-bad DAG fan-out; gemma2_cubecl/dispatch.rs L69-122) + hot-swap-weights-preserve-autotune-caches (LoRA training refreshes weight handles per step while keeping the client + autotune + JIT caches — avoids ~seconds of re-benchmark + recompile per step; gemma4_cubecl/mod.rs L215-235) + tree-reduction-depth-over-sequential-accumulate (plane tree depth ≈77 vs sequential GEMM depth 2304: ~30× better accuracy, prevents 26-layer gradient divergence — perf-neutral kernel choice changing only summation order; gemma2_cubecl/dispatch.rs L881-893, Issue 424 fix). Eleven candidates dropped as duplicates/adjacent (notably: fused triple QKV = Batch 18 precedent "composition of fuse-gate-up-gemv-activation"; offset-aware rope = Batch 14 precedent "section-view is pointer arithmetic"; norm+residual fusion = fuse-add-rms-norm; GEMV→GEMM = gemv-to-small-gemm-m-widening; rope-table hoist = precompute-scalar-reduction; speculative decoding = model-level).
Batch 26 (Research 027): 8 rules — **thirteenth batch mining riir-gpu (forward/ + buffer.rs — the wgpu forward-orchestration cluster, the first HOST-SIDE batch: the dispatch discipline around the kernels, not the kernels themselves)**. Per-slot-uniform-buffer-write-ordering (wgpu flushes ALL `queue.write_buffer` calls before the encoder executes — N dispatches in one encoder sharing ONE uniform buffer all read the LAST write; one 64-byte uniform per slot, each wired into its own pre-built bind group; the Issues 366/367/368 corruption lineage; forward/mod.rs L141-148 + L361-390, forward_buffers.rs L550-557) + derive-params-from-global-invocation-id (encode the per-item parameter in the dispatch grid + decode in-kernel: one dispatch of n_head × seq_len workgroups with causal masking + GQA mapping computed inside — zero per-position uniform writes, zero per-position submits, vs the encoder-per-position fallback; batched_dispatch.rs L280-350, dispatch.rs L316-321) + merge-dispatches-into-single-compute-pass (dependent dispatches queued in ONE compute pass execute IN ORDER — the consumer reads the producer's writes; 6 LoRA dispatches in one pass, input saves riding the copy channel with no pass at all; adapter_merge.rs L410-424 + L654-770) + batched-downloads-single-sync-cycle (N readbacks share ONE encoder/submit/map_async/poll via contiguous 4-byte-aligned staging regions — sync overhead N → 1, ~1.28 ms per measured sync; buffer.rs L263-280, Issue 421 P0.5) + chunk-encoder-submits-below-driver-limit (Metal DEADLOCKS past a compute-pass-per-encoder ceiling — chunk to ~48 passes/encoder, submit each chunk IMMEDIATELY, env-tunable chunk size; forward/mod.rs L1403-1411 + L1456-1458) + prebuilt-bind-groups-fixed-buffer-combos (bind groups bind BUFFERS not data — fixed combos are constants: [n_layer][n_head] attention BGs + [n_layer×6] adapter BGs built once at init, ~768 `create_bind_group` calls per forward → 0; forward_buffers.rs L530-548 + L702-703) + host-cache-immutable-device-weights (constants on the GPU needed host-side every call are cloned ONCE at init — per-call download was the #1 forward bottleneck at 2 GPU sync stalls; embedding gather = row slice + add, uploaded once; forward/mod.rs L111-114 + L869-873 + L1270-1288) + defer-readback-until-after-submit (downloading mid-encoder-construction reads PRE-submission stale state + the readback's internal submit breaks the in-flight encoder — RECORD corrections, submit, then read fresh + apply + upload back; forward/mod.rs L291-295 + L1173-1183, adapter_merge.rs L576-578). Eleven candidates dropped as duplicates/adjacent (notably: SGMV offset tables = grouped-launch-cumsum-offsets family; WebGPU aliasing temp-copyback = pool-buffer-aliasing-dedicated-output family, third variant; batched forward = M-widening composition + grid-derived params; LoRA A→B fusion = gate-up fusion + dual-output; two-phase KV prefill = canonical prefill split).
Total: 177 rules after Batch 26.

Batch 27 (Research 028): 6 rules — **fourteenth batch mining riir-gpu (backward/ + backward_dflash/ — the backward-pass orchestration clusters, completing the host-orchestration trilogy: Batch 26 mined the forward discipline, this batch the backward discipline)**. Staging-packed-uniforms-encoder-ordered-copies (the complementary fix to per-slot buffers: pack ALL uniform params into ONE 16-byte-aligned staging vec, upload once, distribute via `encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer` immediately before each consuming dispatch — the encoder timeline lands each copy exactly between dispatches, so ONE shared uniform can change value per dispatch; backward/mod.rs L15 + L1293-1295 + L1505-1512, staging.rs L31-38) + identical-uniforms-share-single-slot (the EXEMPTION to the write-ordering hazard: sharing is safe IFF every write is byte-identical — the last write is idempotent with all earlier ones; N uniforms + N bind groups → 1, push-once-reuse-offset in staging terms; backward/mod.rs L192-196 + L1447-1452, dispatch.rs L315-320) + persistent-zeros-source-encoder-copy-clear (re-zeroing accumulators between dispatches needs encoder-timeline ordering — a device buffer zeroed once at init (COPY_SRC) + copy clears; queue writes flush before the whole encoder so layer 1 would accumulate on top of layer 0's values; wgpu has no fill API so the zeros source IS the fill; backward/mod.rs L235-241 + L267-268, dispatch.rs L621-631 + L1106-1110, dflash L309-310 + L618 + L1216-1218; THREE-WAY independent subagent convergence) + fuse-sibling-target-grads-single-kernel (N sibling targets sharing the same two backward kernels fuse per KERNEL KIND, not per target: one summed d_input = Σ Rᵢᵀ·gᵢ in-kernel (no combine-add pass) + one grid-sized N × blocks × per_block param-grad dispatch, all adapters + the shared saved input in ONE wide bind group; 6 → 2; dispatch.rs L678-685 + L707 + L849-851) + per-layer-strided-grad-destination (when a consumer needs per-layer partitioned gradients, write layer-major strided offsets directly from the SAME dispatches — the split falls out for free, no second pass, no de-interleave; dflash L205-207 + L1533-1541) + snapshot-transient-grad-for-deferred-download (a host-needed gradient living in an overwrite-doomed temp is pinned by an encoder-ordered copy into a persistent per-adapter buffer, downloaded after submit — the LIFETIME complement to defer-readback's TIMING rule; dispatch.rs L885-897, mod.rs L315-319). Twenty candidates dropped as duplicates/adjacent with documented precedents (notably: batched attention flatten = derive-params-from-global-invocation-id, the exact backward instantiation; weight-grad reduction over seq = weight-grad-batched-outer-product; chunked submits + merge-passes + per-slot keys + two-tier BGs + GPU-resident pipeline = Batch 26/14 enrichments; RO+RW alias copy-back = pool-buffer-aliasing family, FOURTH variant; outer product as n=1 GEMM = conv-1x1-as-matmul; recompute-from-stashed-inputs = activation-recompute-budget; workgroup-resident softmax backward = single-plane-sufficient-reduction; gpu_label! re-flagged and re-dropped per the Batch 26 rust_perf verdict). Mining also surfaced a SUSPECTED LIVE BUG in backward_dflash (uniform_attn_bwd_concat[h] + uniform_lora_a written via queue.write_buffer DURING encoder build → with chunk_size=2 all but the last position per chunk read the wrong query_pos; half-migrated staging fix present at L1042-1057) — filed as a riir-ai issue.
Batch 28 (Research 029): 10 rules — **fifteenth batch mining riir-gpu (deltanet_cubecl.rs + deltanet_chunked_cubecl.rs — the deltanet CubeCL cluster: decode-time recurrence + chunkwise-parallel prefill, the two largest single files in the crate)**. Row-owned-register-blocked-recurrence (G — rows of the state matrix are independent within one decode step: one workgroup per (head, row), the row register-blocked into 4 strided columns per lane, all five algebra steps on registers between ONE global read + ONE write — 4R+2W → 1R+1W, ~3× cut of the memory-bound gating quantity, 32× occupancy headroom; deltanet_cubecl.rs L255-294 + L264-267, Issue 619) + workgroup-equals-plane-native-reduction (E — size the workgroup to EXACTLY one hardware plane and compensate width via register blocking: the native shuffle reduction covers the full logical reduction, broadcasts for free, zero threadgroup memory/barriers — the inverse of the size-to-data-width choice whose reduction-scoped-smaller-than-workgroup hazard is Issue 610; L256-262 + L356-363) + smem-broadcast-scalar-then-parallel-scatter (E — the Issue 616 fix: after a workgroup reduction the leader publishes the scalar through the already-reduced slot, ONE barrier, then ALL threads scatter their own column — 1 global write per thread instead of head_dim serial writes with 127 threads idle; L165-180) + token-flattened-elementwise-modulo-shared-weights (D — per-token elementwise launches flatten [p, n_head] into ONE 1-D index space with per-head weight tables indexed i % n_head: 6144 dispatches → 48 at P=128, each launch's ~25 CPU allocations amortized away, bit-identical by independence; L922-993, Issue 637 T5/638) + fused-conv-state-shift-dot-silu (C — the decode conv1d shifts its sliding window IN PLACE, convolves from the freshly shifted state, applies SiLU, writes back over the input — one kernel, zero intermediates, state persists across steps; L398-448) + softplus-piecewise-saturation-clamp (B — ln(1+e^x) saturates: x for x > 20, 0 for x < -20, transcendental only in the safe band; thresholds pinned to the CPU reference for GPU/CPU bit-comparability; one inf poisons the persistent decayed state permanently; L863-874 + L970-980) + reverse-running-decay-weight (G — the intra-chunk pairwise decay matrix dematerialized into ONE running scalar carried by the consumer loop running j from t DOWN to 0: the empty product (=1 at j=t) anchors the recursion, each step multiplies α[j] after use — the exact inverse of cumsum-gate-to-decay-matrix; deltanet_chunked_cubecl.rs L640-712) + split-decay-outputs-eliminate-readback (D — compact packing is a pessimization when it breaks consumer layouts: write each component directly into the buffer shape its consumer reads and the device→host→device unpack round trip disappears; L342-349, the pipeline test L1949-1969 documents the eliminated hack) + carry-dual-layout-dead-slot (L — the prefill carry producer parameterized (stride + offset) writes the decode consumer's native sliding-window layout directly, leaving slot 0 stale because the decode shift provably overwrites it before any read; L77-98) + nonlinearity-forces-raw-input-buffers (D — the negative space of in-place aliasing: a sequential in-place producer trick is incompatible with batched execution when a nonlinearity intervenes (later tokens' windows read earlier tokens' POST-activation values); separate raw RO inputs + WO outputs are the correctness-enabling shape; L27-47). Sixteen candidates dropped as duplicates/adjacent with documented precedents. Mining also confirmed a PRODUCTION BUG (cross-chunk plane_sum mixing partials of different columns → block-constant output masked by test tolerance; production-wired via prefill_deltanet_recurrence_chunked) + 3 latent/edge bugs (legacy recurrence head_dim≠128 fallback broken; partial-chunk carry GPU-vs-CPU divergence; GPU L2-normalize missing the CPU zero-norm guard) — filed as a riir-ai issue.
Batch 29 (Research 030): 10 rules — **sixteenth batch mining riir-gpu (deltanet_input_proj_fused.rs + epilogue/ — the decode-path fusion cluster: Issue 602 sibling-GEMV fan-out + Plan 106 Track 3 CODA epilogue fusion)**. Defer-readback-gated-by-live-output-size (A — deferral is NOT unconditional: 5 concurrent ~70 KB FFN buffers measured 16% SLOWER via memory pressure while ~8 KB of input-projection outputs chain freely — gate on total live bytes; the failure boundary the existing deferral rules lacked) + weight-handle-cache-keyed-by-allocation-identity (A — a hook receiving CPU weight refs resolves to cached device handles via the allocation's (pos, neg) pointer pair in a lock-free map, with an on-the-fly miss path; the keying keeps the cache invisible to the API surface) + first-readback-pays-queue-drain (D — after one dispatch burst the first read drains the queue and later reads return immediately: syncs are a function of BURSTS, not read calls — no defensive per-read syncs, no batched-download machinery needed) + epilogue-at-register-write-phase (C — the CODA insight: memory-bound elementwise ops execute while the GEMV output is still in REGISTERS, riding lane 0's single store after plane_sum; 2→1 dispatches per site, 52/26 layers, 104 combined) + delay-rms-scale-past-gemv-epilogue (C — the commutation RMSNorm(x)@W = r·(x·γ)@W splits the norm into extract/γ/defer with the file's own honest 3→3 accounting: the win is the last-GEMV fusion + eliminated normed-vector write, and with handle-to-handle passing the dominant benefit is dispatch overhead, NOT bandwidth) + pack-scalar-params-into-combined-buffer (D — the ~4-5 Array-param budget met by ONE packed f32 params buffer, derived values host-computed, u32s bitcast through the pack, budget coupled via total_array_count metadata) + enum-dispatch-separate-kernel-per-variant (M — no generics in #[cube] kernels → host-level #[repr(u8)] enum + ONE branch-free kernel per variant + metadata-driven plumbing) + epilogue-must-be-workgroup-local (E — fusion legality is data OWNERSHIP scope, not just dependency: attention stays 9 dispatches while post-attn drops to 1; cross-workgroup consumers cannot be epilogued) + launch-unchecked-caller-owned-size-contract (D — elide per-launch validation into a documented # Safety size contract at the launcher seam; 14×26 microsecond dispatches) + fast-math-contraction-behind-feature-flag (B — a cfg_attr pair on a dedicated Cargo feature keeps strict-FP the byte-identical default while the dot loop contracts to FMA only on the opt-in build). Thirteen candidates dropped as duplicates/adjacent (notably: the fan-out/chaining/host-cache/defer-timing family fully carried by Batches 19/21/26; smem broadcast + tree reduction + grid-stride exact instances; NormResidual = fuse-add-rms-norm; the Option<&Hook> seam dropped as adoption architecture, not kernel mechanism; DispatchBudget checkpoints dropped as methodology). Mining also surfaced 2 latent cache hazards (pointer-key staleness on model reload; key covers only qkv, not z/a/b) + a stale-doc-numbers bug (epilogue/mod.rs L43 '18 → 14, ~22%' vs pinned 19→14, 26.32%) — filed as riir-ai Issue 674.

Ragged-pack-offset-table-fused-dispatch (D — the six per-adapter GEMV dispatches repacked into contiguous buffers + a prefix-sum offset table so ONE launch over Σ out_dim covers every adapter; ragged segments need an offset table, a modulo cannot identify them) + twin-projection-shared-delta-adapter (L — GQA K/V shape-twins share ONE adapter pair: adapters 6→5 per layer, params −33%, KV delta cache halves at inference) + min-rank-inert-adapter-zero-alpha (M — structurally-required-but-inert adapters allocate rank=1 + alpha=0: minimum legal footprint, annihilated by scale not contents) + debug-only-gpu-label-macro (M — cfg-split label macro: debug gets format!'d capture labels, release gets a typed None constant) + sync-map-result-stack-cell (A — poll(Wait) IS the barrier, so the map callback parks its result in an UnsafeCell instead of Arc<Mutex>: zero lock + zero alloc on the readback critical path) + staging-reuse-grow-only-map-prefix (A — one loop-owned grow-only MAP_READ staging buffer, ~50-500μs driver alloc amortized to zero steady-state, mapping only the copied prefix; merged from two subagent proposals) + mapped-at-creation-init-upload (A — one-shot weight uploads via create_buffer_init: no encoder, no submit, no queue-timeline ordering hazard; the tell is the unused _queue param) + dtype-normalize-once-at-upload (N — bf16→f32 is a pure bit-shift; normalize ONCE at ingestion instead of per-dispatch forever). Batch 32a (Research 034): 11 rules — **nineteenth batch mining riir-gpu (the edge_lora CORE cluster: edge_module.rs + types.rs + training.rs + adaptive_width.rs — host-side training orchestrators; first half of the edge_lora module per the 32a/32b split, 32b = self-play/arena/consolidation remains)**. inactive params zero BY CONSTRUCTION, no masks, no atomics — the host-side shape of MoE backward) + inplace-grad-accumulate-deferred-postscale (B — accumulate RAW per sample into one buffer, ONE post-loop reciprocal sweep: 1 division total, not batch×params; n]` on a FALSE aliasing premise (read buffer ≠ write buffer): write the destination directly; genuine ping-pong temps are loop-carried scratch state) + scoped-init-at-consume-site (A — blanket whole-scratch clear memsets dead lanes + buffers the consumer re-zeroes anyway: only ACCUMULATION targets need pre-zeroing, scoped to the live window) + monomorphize-per-element-trait-calls (B — per-node `&dyn` vtable indirection → generic `+ ?Sized`; same seed rebuilds the identical encoder) + latent-encode-at-capture (A — snapshot the latent encoding at capture instead of buffering raw states: peak memory moves × hidden_dim; no post-hoc drift; the GPU-side compress-at-the-boundary discipline) + non-saturating-outcome-reward-floor (B — raw 0/1 outcomes put losing episodes' gradient weight at exactly zero; 0.15 + 0.70·clamp keeps losses teaching "avoid this direction") + collect-then-resolve-snapshot-batch (E — simultaneous-turn loops collect ALL selections against the frozen pre-tick snapshot (read-only, order-independent, parallelizable) then resolve in fixed order — no information leak; encode BEFORE/AFTER rides the same structure) + timeout-scalar-tiebreak-decisive-outcome (D — a capped game falls through to a maintained-scalar (HP) tiebreak instead of a flat draw: signal density at zero maintenance cost; Batch 41 (Research 045): 6 rules — **thirtieth batch mining riir-gpu (kimi_k3_gpu_backward_sequence.rs, 1,328 LOC — the KimiK3 backward SEQUENCE: the PRODUCTION full-model backward caller path (Issue 390 T6.7) + weight_buffer_cache.rs, 459 LOC — the persistent weight-buffer write substrate; the ORIGIN of the B39 hotswap-cache pattern, never itself mined)**. Concurrent-disjoint-write-buffer-parallelizes-uploads (E — a large host-to-device upload serialized on one thread is host-bandwidth-bound, not device-bound; native wgpu backends do NOT serialize `queue.write_buffer` calls targeting disjoint offsets, so one big upload splits into 256-aligned chunks written concurrently from rayon workers + N independent per-unit updates parallelize under `rayon::scope` with a first-error Mutex collector; on cuda_backend (extract_buffer = no-op Ok) EVERY update takes the severing fallback; L158-161 + L128-132 + sequence L478-481/L527-535) + pool-slot-write-coalescing-owns-only-declared-ranges (A — the writer-side complement of B40's build-side rule: when N cached slots are pool sub-allocations, group by parent buffer, sort by offset, merge exact-adjacency runs into ONE staging copy + ONE write (~246 calls/step -> a handful) — but a coalesced write may cover ONLY the union of declared slot ranges: gap bytes may belong to OTHER pool tenants; drop it and the manager re-issues the range under the still-alive view, silently aliasing a different tenant's bytes; the handle field looks vestigial but deleting it corrupts unrelated tensors — the OPPOSITE direction of B21's pool-movement staleness; L40-43/L56-62) + gpu-type-bridge-borrows-not-clones (A — bridging CPU reference types to GPU module types by cloning saved activations multiplies traffic per layer per step; the worst shape clones TWICE (owned copy of the source collection, then a converter that field-clones again — the first clone 100% dead when the converter takes &T); GPU "backward saved" types are frequently field-identical to the CPU types: borrow; where the callee's owned-slice API forces the clone, fix the API, not the clone; sequence L892-896/L1082-1095/L941-943/L1179-1227) + superseded-primitive-machinery-zombie (A — when a composition path replaces a per-module GPU primitive (per-token GEMV -> CPU batched primitives), the superseded primitive's support machinery (pre-built handle, per-step transpose + re-upload pipeline, caches, staging buffers) keeps executing every step because its lifecycle hangs off a long-lived struct's update schedule; Batch 42 (Research 046): 4 rules — **thirty-first batch mining riir-gpu (gemma2_d2f/mod.rs, 1,579 LOC — the Gemma-2 D2F block-causal decode, CubeCL GPU path, Plan 108: a CPU-orchestrated hybrid forward whose every dispatch result is read back to CPU + every input re-uploaded per call + KV in a CPU CpuKVCache, cloned from the legacy dispatch shape instead of consuming the in-tree device-resident substrate; + gemma2_d2f_sc.rs, 800 — Self-Conditioning, Plan 250 T1-T4, arXiv:2604.02028)**. Refine-loop-invariant-prefix-elision (D — an iterative refinement loop whose mutation set is a proper subset S must persist the complement: block-causal masking closes the prompt against the block (t_n proof at attention_cubecl L1214-1217) + the loop only rewrites block tokens, so prompt K/V + hidden states are PROVABLY bit-identical across denoise steps — yet each step resets the cache + re-forwards the FULL sequence (the design doc admits it at mod.rs L499-502); forward the prefix ONCE: O(steps x seq) -> O(seq + steps x |S|); precondition: the refine set must be closed — SC conditioning feeding prompt embeddings breaks it) + softmax-entropy-via-shift-identity (B — ln(e_t) = logit_t - max EXACTLY (the shift is exp's argument, so its log is the shift itself): accumulate sum_e_ln_e as e*shift in the same sweep building the softmax denominator + top-3 cascade, recover entropy as ln(S) - (1/S)*sum e*ln(e) — ONE transcendental for the whole distribution, not N; NaN-guard the underflow tail (0*-inf); the file's own SamplerFeatures::from_logits is the POSITIVE instance at L103-127 + L151-154) + runtime-identity-gate-cached-verdict-elides-side-projection (D — a learnable side component with algebraically-neutral init (W_sc = [I|0]) ships a runtime content-classification: the still-neutral verdict computed ONCE per WRITE at the sole setter boundary (re-derived from CONTENTS so externally-supplied buffers are honestly re-classified), cached as a bool, and BOTH the side-input PRODUCTION and the projection CONSUMPTION branch on it to ELIDE work — turning enabled-but-untrained = free from a construction-time hope into a runtime invariant; the completeness requirement is load-bearing: the file's own update_from_logits is gated only by sc_enabled while its only consumer is identity-gated OFF — 2.36 GB/position/step of provably-discarded host work (Issue 695 H16); Batch 49 (Research 055): 1 rule — **the Bench-686 measured-lesson distillation (not file mining — a rule distilled from a measured GOAT landed the same day: riir-ai Issue 706 / Bench 686, the rmsnorm_quantize_f32 single-pass + register-unroll fix on the 4090 cudarc decode path, kernel −18.4% at ppl bit-exact)**. Runtime-arg-loop-bound-demotes-arrays-to-local-memory (A — a correctly-sized per-thread array whose per-element loops are bounded by a RUNTIME kernel arg that is constant at every call site is demoted to local memory: the compiler cannot unroll a runtime-bounded loop and a dynamically-indexed per-thread array has no register form, so every access is a local-memory round-trip and every array the loop touches inherits the demotion; the invariant is a LAUNCHER fact the kernel cannot see — the host passes a Rust const as an int arg — so the fix hoists the bound into the kernel as a compile-time constant + #pragma unroll, keeping the (i < len) tail-guard for partial tiles, with unrolling preserving the ascending accumulation order → bit-identical; the pathology is INVISIBLE by default and surfaces only when a neighboring change adds traffic to the same address space — the naive v1 fix (gamma prefetch) measured 0.6-1% SLOWER in paired interleaved A/B because it traded L1-hot global reads for more local-memory traffic; distinct from B46 declared-max-array-taxed-per-thread-under-runtime-bound on three axes: SIZE-declared-max footprint tax vs BOUND-driven per-access local addressing, exactly-sized array vs oversized, pipeline-override size specialization vs const-bound hoist + unroll; source: cudarc_kernels/mod.rs rmsnorm_quantize_f32 L193-235 current + the pre-fix form at d11a9732e~1; evidence: nsys 5,460.9 → 4,455.7 ns avg across 20,702 launches, same-code-drift controlled, ppl bit-exact 5.4681/3.7778/0.6909). Corpus 343 -> 344 (A 80). No falsifications (single-rule batch; dedup grep zero hits across local.mem|unroll|register|spill|dynamically.index + the B46 three-axis distinction encoded in the rule text). Batch 60 (Research 067, Plan 006): 1 rule — **the SIXTH measured-GOAT distillation after corpus-complete** (reopen path (c), riir-train Issue 460 / katgpt-rs `e2853fbf`, 2026-08-18: the MLA backward reconstructed the pre-gate attention output as `attn_out_gated / gate`; `gate = sigmoid(W_g·h)` underflows to a HARD ZERO in f32 below ~−88 → 0/0 NaN — silent in the forward, surfacing steps later as an opaque `NsInvSqrtFailed`; 4 of 10 real sequences at random init, 4 of 5 seeds). Pre-gate-recovery-by-division-cancels-algebraically (N — never reconstruct a pre-gate activation by dividing the gated output by the gate: every gradient consumer multiplies the pre-gate value by gate-derived factors, so `pre·g·(1−g) == gated·(1−g)` — the factor CANCELS, bit-identical wherever the old form was finite, no epsilon; where g == 0 the value is information-theoretically unrecoverable (0/0 carries no information): guard public reconstructions to 0.0 + SAVE the true value at checkpoint boundaries; detection tell: the NaN localizes to the single weight matrix consuming the reconstruction while every post-gate-reading gradient stays clean. Distinct from B35 (derivative→0, fix = raw recompute), B32b (the recovery-optimization family), B32a (forward sigmoid hygiene)). Bench 475 repro 6/10 → 10/10 usable backward passes. Corpus 363 → 364 (N 21). Batch 166 (Plan 157 / Research 194): 1 rule — **the FWHT butterfly staging ladder (`fwht-butterfly-staging-ladder-warp-smem-register`, B: Compute 65→66; the staged lead PROMOTED by Research 194 after its benchable instance landed in-tree: riir-ai Plan 600 / Issue 980's folded-prefill rotation lane, `bonsai2_hadamard` DEFAULT-ON 2026-09-19)**: for a wide in-block butterfly (W > the all-register budget) stage by the THREE-WAY span ladder — sub-warp spans ride warp shuffles (zero barriers), the genuinely cross-warp spans stage through smem (ONE barrier each, ≤ ~3 at service geometries), and the top stages are REGISTER-RESIDENT on the per-lane contiguous-run view (zero syncs on the most data-heavy stages) — never all-stages-through-smem with a barrier pair per stage (the incumbent: 256 threads, W=1024, `__shared__ float smem[1024]`, all 10 stages, ~20 `__syncthreads`). Fork evidence is EXPECTATION PRICING, not ours: block-per-row W≥512 +17%/+11% decode (9B/2B, H100, folded bands); Hadamard the largest single decode kernel (25.8% of GPU time at block 4096); the #161 Metal twin +4–36% decode at a +2.2–4.8% pp cost (their variant — risk pricing, not transferable). The sign fold is NOT part of the ladder (sign-first load / signs-on-store — already shipped in our kernels, the 09-17 rider (b)(i) retired at intake). The dispatch logic for 5090 batched FP32 is missing entirely"; where the dispatcher works the same template LOSES by 5-11pp; protocol = ncu kernel name + pipe util + same-binary cross-SKU comparison + the vendor's own batched API form as baseline (strided beat a loop of single calls 37.8-43.3% — a win over the loop form is a strawman); recurrence class since 2018 Maxwell + vLLM #35467, blog-flagged) + `sub-ms-bench-boost-clock-never-engages` (D — below ~1 ms per call the boost governor never engages: recipe.yaml excludes 256/512 because "the SM clock fluctuates between idle (~260 MHz) and steady (~2400 MHz) within a single benchmark run"; measured tells 562→1890 MHz (single 1024) and 682→810 MHz with 33/67% variance (batched 256×256×4); protocol = interleaved-pair iterations + iter-0 discard + median + ratio-not-absolute + clock sampling, clocks deliberately UNLOCKED so interleaved ratios stay meaningful) + `lds-cluster-cross-warp-phase-not-static-distance` (B — after static latency hiding suffices (median 40 FFMAs vs ~30-cycle LDS latency, "that hypothesis was wrong"), the LDS temporal distribution governs CROSS-WARP PHASE: cuBLAS clusters LDS back-to-back (spacing 0) vs the generator's spread (spacing 5) → dispatch_stall 44.21% vs 22.36%, short_scoreboard 19.95% vs 11.84%, the FMA-pipe gap maps 1:1; generator-side cause is EMISSION ORDER ("Generate FMA block: B load first, then interleave A load + FMA per row", lower.py:192-198), no scheduling knob exists; boundaries: inline PTX no-change, SASS stall-count patching breaks correctness; diagnose by the kernel's OWN dominant stall — the undersized 128×32 tile fails under mio_throttle 212.82% (LSU saturation), a different mechanism) + `tma-single-issuer-bulk-tile` (A — ONE thread issues the whole 2D tile via cp.async.bulk.tensor.2d against a host-encoded descriptor, the TMA DMA engine writes smem directly, the other 255 threads issue zero load instructions; mbarrier byte-count completion (expect_tx = BOTH operands' bytes) + parity-toggled double-buffer + the per-tile __syncthreads as the barrier-REUSE spacer (without it a slow thread's parity check can mistake the next completion for its own phase); descriptor passing splits by batch: __grid_constant__ by value vs device array indexed by blockIdx.z; honest claim = instruction count (0 st.shared vs cuBLAS's 256) + implementation simplicity (~300 generated lines), NOT FMA pipe (~68% vs 72.9%); CAVEAT in-rule: nt=K/bk integer division silently TRUNCATES ragged K) + `multiplexed-grid-axis-partition-modes-exclusive` (E — split-K and batch both consuming blockIdx.z must be REFUSED at config time: the runner's `grid_z = BATCH > 1 ? BATCH : k_splits_val` silently lets batch win and the k-partitions never launch; the generator's own ValueError carries the rationale; split-K's atomicAdd epilogue obligates per-launch output zeroing as a launch CONTRACT; the exclusivity is also a perf statement — batching already saturates grid.z). RIDERS (7): l2-grouped-tile-schedule += the SWIZ=8 band-of-row-tiles formula + the PADDED-LAUNCHER half (grid_x padded to whole groups, early-return absorbs) + the boundary (swizzle moves DRAM 32%→8.5% blog-only; repo ncu shows post-swizzle DRAM PARITY 9.40 vs 9.80% — scheduling finding, not memory); unroll-fixed-inner += the boundary (partial unroll 4/8/16 monotonicWORSE, no-unroll 57% of cuBLAS, tuned middle 93-95%); autotune-warps-stages += launch_bounds(256,2) 20× SLOWER (5.7 KB spill from the 128-register clamp) with the FAMILY CAVEAT (a tensor-pipe kernel ships min_blocks=2 positive) + triple-buffer same-IPC ceiling; cpasync-multistage-tile-pipeline += the TMA crossover (cooperative cp.async 82.6% vs TMA 93-95%, per-thread ISSUE cost is the axis) + mbarrier.try_wait exits-on-first-read note; hardcoded-fast-path-jit-specialization += the codegen twin (fresh .cu per (M,N,K), #define dims + #if-aligned-bounds epilogue arms IN THE SAME SOURCE; honest magnitude 98→101%, common-sizes-only scope); launch-heuristic-pinned-to-calibration-arch += three sharpenings (compute capability is NOT a sufficient SKU key — name-regex dispatch because 5090 and Pro 6000 share sm_120; the tile optimum tracks the DMA GENERATION (TM=28 sm_120 vs TM=8 sm_90); a same-CC divergence can be LIBRARY-side — attribute before tuning); fast-math-contraction-behind-feature-flag += the flag-pair SPLIT (--fmad=true is the EXACT half, single rounding, kept ON unconditionally; --use_fast_math carries ftz/relaxed-div and is refused; without fmad SGEMM throughput halves).
