ONE own rule: shard-granularity-lcm-never-cut-rotation-block (L — the SHARD-ALIGNMENT law family: a shard boundary respects EVERY block constraint on the axis — 'Split blocks for Hadamard-rotated weights use lcm(type block, rotation block) so shards never cut a rotation block'; the axis-survival reshape 4D [n, ne0/n, ne1, ne2*ne3] 'so the split axis survives the rotation' + the asserted ne0 % n == 0; views of a grouped-GDN output inherit the parent's split scaled by their own geometry; elementwise MUL/ADD/SUB/DIV over a feature-sharded activation slices its full-width mirrored operand per shard, ADD_ID excluded; the nr[0] single-segment repeat refinement + the mirrored-x-axis≥2 case). RIDER: the PLATFORM-KEYED verify-cost face on cost-aware-verify-length-survival-cost-table (#354's MTP half — 'On CUDA the cost of a verify batch grows faster with the draft depth than on Metal'; depth-2 default when draft-mtp + CUDA offload + --spec-draft-n-max unset, 'An explicit --spec-draft-n-max always wins'). LANE INTEL: the fork now serves OUR league model Bonsai 2 27B PQ2_0/Q2_0 under 2-GPU tensor split (2x H200, mean KLD 2.5e-4, top-1 99.3%; single-GPU + Qwen3-4B/3.5-4B bit-identical to base) — no prism-b tag (ladder top 10777), no ggml-cuda absorption movement, the bout trigger NOT fired. Gates: batch246 needle+parse + the rider needle gate; kernel_opt lib 3133/0/1i; clippy ×3 postures clean (7 doc_markdown healed via cargo refine, the B192 class); retrieval floors HELD (blended 90.6/94.2/95.3 vs floors 89.4/93.6/93.6, kernel self-variants 100.0%, 58.55 s, NO re-pin). The NULL record: #362 (tests: portable popcount — test-portability, no law) + the upstream envelope `b76b1337`→`2f729cfa` (Muse Spark Open model support + lightonocr mtmd — model-registration class, no corpus host).

Batch 242 (Plan 244 / Research 274, 2026-10-15 — the mlx-serve GDN/QSA pack, the oMLX row's designated follow-up pool lead): corpus 816→847 (+31 — the corpus's LARGEST single-source batch; A 167→174, B 88→91, D 191→200, E 77→79, K 15→16, L 20→21, N 66→74) + SIX count-neutral riders — **source ddalcu/mlx-serve @ pin `1015f7854fd8ee5c22e34993aaddee2443b9cfbf` (MIT, 2026-10-14; a native Zig LLM serving engine for Apple Silicon — MLX + GGUF, embedded MSL kernels in Zig string literals; a PORTS HUB with in-source citations to TensorFold/mlxfast/oMLX/sushi/PrismML, every port axis dedup-adjudicated at the verdict)**. Three slices over disjoint source files, three authoring subagents + coordinator wiring + coordinator spot-verification of the load-bearing quotes (gdn_decode's prework header, the decline-latch pair, the GOAT prescale). Slice 97 (GDN/QSA/RHT core, 10): split-prework-recompute-beats-inter-kernel-barrier (D — THREE placement regimes for one per-head prework keyed on sharing structure; the file-header law 'cheaper than a barrier between kernels'; the fold-depth rider + the read-amplify PAIR named) + resource-limit-first-dispatch-probe-decline-latch (A — first-dispatch-as-probe, per-width latch, ERROR-STRING discrimination: 'maximum allowed threads per threadgroup' declines / 'Any other error stays latched'; the declined-shape face folded) + read-amplify-vs-sync-row-ceiling-admission (A — the fused row-reduction+consumer re-reads the whole row per threadgroup: decode/verify widths only) + decay-gate-dtype-one-point-zero-lock (N — 'a bf16 gate near one is exactly 1.0 and that head never forgets'; the gate dtype follows the STATE under bf16; league-relevant, our GDN state-dtype lane) + spec-tree-parents-first-walk-register-restart-states (E — the tree protocol in ONE launch: kept[TL][4] register snapshots at host-flagged restart rows; the commit-replay + host-table faces folded; the fold-does-not-cover-trees anti-lead carried) + op-chain-collapse-membership-binary-search-mask (D — 8 dependent launches → 1 via binary-search membership over a sentinel-padded sorted selection) + partial-softmax-l-zero-empty-split-skip-flag (N) + gqa-shared-channel-block-write-once-owner (A — 'if (part == 0 && (sg == 2 || hv % GRP == 0))' — one duplication-axis writer AND one sharing-axis owner) + joined-projection-in-place-stride-consumption (A) + device-keyed-threadgroup-size-row-independence (D). Slice 98 (quant matmul + packs, 9): masked-in-place-field-prescale-other-operand (N — THE GOAT: consume a packed field WITHOUT shifting it out of the word — mask in place, pre-scale the OTHER operand by 2^-shift via the f32 bit pattern `as_type<float>(uint(127 - ...) << 23)`; the 6-bit straddle exception stated in-source; portable CPU-exact, NOVEL numerics) + admission-clones-upstream-dispatch-for-bit-order (D — 'Take only the calls MLX sends to its sorted row-block kernel (B >= 16 && B / E >= 4)... MTP verify rounds must keep its bits'; the per-expert rungs face folded) + row-count-invariant-bits-split-keyed-on-weight-shape (B — 'K slices for an (n, k) weight: fixed by the shape, never by the row count' so 'a drafted window verifies with the one-row step's bits') + threadgroup-cap-halving-with-order-preserving-sum (D — cap-failure-triggered simdgroup halving, memoized per (n,k,bits,rt); bit-safe ONLY because fewer simdgroups walk the S chunks in the same fixed order) + canary-admission-per-config-bit-equal-stock (D — runtime synthetic-workload bit-compare vs stock, cached per config key; the reference ORACLE is regime-keyed; 'self-test failed; original kept') + prompt-form-refused-toolchain-format-gap-reopen-trigger (D — the negative-row doctrine exemplar: measured 0.87-0.93×, root cause no `uint2b_format`, 'Re-test if `uint2b_format` ever appears') + per-generation-plan-table-unmeasured-generation-fallback (K — measured on the Bonsai 27B shapes, OUR league model) + quad-lane-line-load-shuffle-word-exchange-ring (A — 'uint4 ring[2][4];') + tp-rank-inferred-from-scale-grid-ambiguity-refused (L — 'return found orelse error.InvalidQkvGeometry;' — ambiguity refuses, never guesses). Slice 99 (attention + oracles, 12): tree-attention-partial-share-tail-walk-ordered-merge (E — PARTIAL → TAIL → MERGE; the node == serial one-row step contract test-pinned) + lossy-route-physical-error-bar-from-noise-floor (N — 'a ratio against it compares to near zero and is not a bar. The physical bar is relative error against the output itself' — the Issue-750-T3 lossy law's external validation #3) + finiteness-before-diff-nan-blind-comparison-hole (N — 'the composed causal arm shipped NaN for months behind exactly this hole. Finiteness is asserted BEFORE the diff.'; FOUR identical sites) + bit-equality-oracle-keyed-on-reference-dispatch (N — the test RE-IMPLEMENTS the reference's dispatch predicate to split the oracle: bit-assert where determinate, no-worse-than-truth where the reference splits K) + gqa-reshape-q-down-never-expand-packed-kv (A — reshape the dense operand DOWN, a pure relabel, never materialize the packed bank ×g) + bit-equal-fusion-mirrors-reference-reduction-regime (N — mirror the framework's parallel decomposition AND its own width-regime boundary; the rounding-point-placement second half) + shared-transform-memo-on-underlying-descriptor-identity (D — keyed on the shape-DESCRIPTOR pointer, not the wrapper handle; retained-handle anti-alias; the int8 sibling memo merged) + row-bucket-threadgroup-staging-budget-decline (A — staging sized by row BUCKET 1/4/16, 16 KB ceiling, decline-not-fail) + strided-interleave-split-ordered-merge-serial-reference (B) + gqa-head-position-row-packing-keys-read-once (B) + config-cache-keyed-full-layout-not-row-count (D) + where-not-multiply-additive-mask-inf (N — IEEE Inf/NaN semantics: additive -inf via where-not-multiply). RIDERS (6, count-neutral): the 2-bit per-j magic-exponent LADDER 2^(10-2j) → lop3-int4-to-half2-dequant (qmv2.zig h2dec) + the per-step StT rounding DECODE-side twin → mid-prefill-recurrent-state-capture-boundary-unsplit (gdn_decode.zig:242 'The next token reads the state serial decoding stored (StT), not the f32.') + the sigtab-TABLE-AS-FUSION-ENABLER → exact-bf16-domain-activation-lut (deepseek_v4.zig + moe_affine4.zig) + the FRAMEWORK-DERIVED T-ladder (f32 → T(acc) → T-typed product → ascending T-add) → moe-router-weight-fold-into-reduce (transformer.zig:42778+ 'MLX's bf16 sum reduction accumulates in the INPUT dtype — the moeRouterTopK lesson') + the packed-KV views-in-place runtime instance → quantized-stored-bytes-not-widened-patterns (transformer.zig:15790+ 'a contiguous copy erases the win') + the COMPOSED-LIFT fold (e2m1 `<< 9` + e4m3 `<< 7`, both lifted by ONE 2^22 post-simd_sum multiply) → minifloat-normal-code-shift-bias-diff-decode (moe_fp4.zig). ONE rider SKIPPED honestly: the in-buffer sole-reader tile-repack face (host derived-pack-immutable-original-content-addressed-atomic) does NOT exist in the source (grep across gather_qmm_nax.zig + kernels/*.metal: tiles stage in ephemeral threadgroup scratch, never repacking a persistent buffer) — never-invent. Dispositions: 1 killed (dequant-dtype-follows-scales-not-hardcoded, C+ — 3 lines of mechanism), 3 folded in-rule + per-expert folded into admission-clones. LANDED ABOVE the 12-16 estimate — the sources were richer than the ~50% falsification factor predicted (2 killed/folded of 33 candidates), disclosed per the lithos precedent. Lane intel: LEAGUE INTEL HIGH — the league model family's serving mechanics at depth (the GDN decode/verify/tree protocol, the QSA 128-bit K/V staging, per-generation tables measured on OUR Bonsai 27B). Gates: batch242 needle+parse (31 rules × needles + the pin + the batch tag + both pseudocode halves parse; + batch242_riders 6-host needle gate); corpus 404/404; kernel_opt lib 3126/0/1i; default clippy + kernel_opt + kernel_opt,latent_retrieval clippy ×3 clean (6 doc_markdown healed via cargo refine, the B192 class); retrieval floors HELD 21/21 (297 s, no re-pin — kernel self-variants 100%, blended above floors); freeze/score-bench NOT owe

Record: Plan 244 / Research 274. GOAT BENCH (2026-10-16, plan 246 / Bench 117 / bench_008, the rule's own benchability clause discharged): G1 988 cases BIT-IDENTICAL across all four arms (4-bit + 8-bit + the 2-bit ALU twin + the 6-bit straddle exception; edge words + the ±2^-98 regime boundary — |x·2^-off| >= 2^-126 the disclosed in-regime bound); G2 SPLIT at 25 shapes × 3 faces: the byte-aligned faces WIN (the 2-bit ALU twin 1.28-1.34× — the ladder also folds the per-element (x·sv) multiply into staging; 8-bit 1.07-1.10× — the naive shift-down pays scalar-gather loads + lane-repacking shuffles while the byte-aligned mask is a shuffle-free pand) and the 4-bit half-lane face is the measured CPU ANTI-CASE 0.87-0.94× (asm-root-caused: the naive psrld-imm + cvtdq2ps lowering beats the masked form's exponent-trick convert chain — the u32-to-f32 of exponent-region values costs por 0x53000000/subps/addps; re-armed on a host/toolchain move); G4 zero-alloc; the op-count ledger 2,097,152 hot-loop shifts eliminated for 4,096 staged multiplies (512:1). LANE INTEL: LLVM's own lowering of the masked convert builds the float from the exponent field via por/subps bit patterns — the backend independently discovered the rule's technique.

Batch 247 (Plan 251 / Research 279, 2026-10-16 — the splash runtime-oracle rider pair, COUNT-NEUTRAL): corpus STANDS 848 — **source incoai/splash (Apache-2.0) @ `4919202` (PR #428) + `3903390`, read in full at the vendor clone fetched `be3b083c5`; every quote verbatim at the pins**. TWO riders on `mid-prefill-recurrent-state-capture-boundary-unsplit` (entries_gpu_95, the B236 capture rule's own VERIFICATION half — the mechanism half untouched): (a) the KV-ALIKE oracle gate (`4919202`) — the captured-state check 'held them to 0.999 whenever their FFNs ran alike', which 'assumed the GPU computes a row the same whatever the command's row count'; but 'GGUF float projections (#403 loads BF16 and F16 tensors as such) pick the neural accelerator tile or the simdgroup one by row count; both are fp32 up to accumulation order', so the 1632-row capturing command and the 544-/1056-row ended ones picked different tiles and 'ISTA-DASLab and Swift-1.5 Qwen3.8-27B GSQ-RCO IQ3_S failed test-real at --ane-ffn-share 0 (continued_conv_cos 0.963)' — an ORACLE artifact, never a kernel defect; the fix: 'The check now runs where the ended commands' KV of the rows both computed equals the capturing command's bit for bit, which covers a split that ran in one and not the other as well, and prints kv_alike instead of ffns_alike' — the like-arithmetic precondition is itself a CHECKED PREDICATE, never an assumption. (b) the RETAINED-ROWS CONTENT oracle (`3903390`, the Research-268 'content-vs-count' deferral discharged) — 'the retained rows were compared nowhere' in the budget-vs-mask commit check; the fix compares 'the two lanes' GDN cells and the rows each retained, byte for byte, so that the next step of either lane would fold the same state', reading completed lanes' rows where they wait via `model::Runtime::pendingGdnRows`; the seeded-defect witness: one bf16-ulp row 'fails the check at its first count ... where main's oracle compares no rows' — count-equality is NOT state-equality. THE PAIR = the oracle's two failure directions on ONE host: UNDER-compared (counts without content) + OVER-compared (content across unlike partitions without a bit-alike gate) — together: compare content, gated on bit-alikeness; the third member of the oracle-law family (B233 content-vs-shape; B242 oracle-keyed-on-dispatch; this: the boundary-state oracle's own soundness). Gates: batch247 needle+parse FIRST RUN GREEN (17 needles + corpus==848 + both halves parse); kernel_opt lib 3134/0/1i; default lib 2678/2678; clippy ×2 postures 0 errors; retrieval floors HELD 21/21 @312.88 s (blended 90.6/94.2/95.3, byte-identical to B246's read — NO re-pin); freeze/score-bench NOT owed (P30); binary NOT rebuilt (kernel_opt not in the release set). Envelope context: splash `ffc2d4be`→`be3b083c5` (+7: #416 weights-release honesty WATCH C+, #419 docs NULL, #418 = P60 consumed); llama.cpp envelope `2f729cfa`→`59d5fad7c` = 2 NULL Granite convert commits, NEW PIN `59d5fad7c`.

Batch 261 (Plan 265 / Research 297, 2026-10-20 -- the PrismML fork prism-b10787 window, the standing-watch fork leg FIRED): corpus 920->923 (A 186->187 `prefill-batch-pool-f16-conversion-sized-by-actual-length` -- PrismML `25f68a4` #330: `GGML_CUDA_FA_PREFILL_F16=N` -- prefill-sized batches (>8 queries) over a quantized K/V cache of at most N cells convert K/V to f16 in POOL memory sized by the ACTUAL KV length and run the f16 MMA kernel, decode keeps the in-place quantized read, NOTHING reserved at load ('the reserved f16 copy would be n_ctx cells'), the alloc accounting treats pool-backed as 'nothing to reserve beyond dst', and the in-place kernel's supported-predicate REFUSES when the route armed (disjoint by the same query-count conjunct); the boundary predicate is the AMORTIZATION economics; off by default; D 220->221 `dtype-branch-dispatch-parity-in-place-precondition` -- PrismML `ec1ee3a` #307: the quantized branch of the Ada kernel-choice ladder lacked the F16 branch's GQA>4 ratio rule, 1.21-2.95x at long context on THE LEAGUE MODEL (Ternary Bonsai 2 27B PTQ1_0, GQA 6, q4_0 K/V, head 256, RTX 4070: 44.8->54.2 @32k, 25.3->41.2 @120k, mtp 53.3->84.0 / 23.2->68.5; range computed from the commit's own rows); TWO halves: sibling dispatcher branches carry PARITY on measured admission rules + the widening carries the IN-PLACE PRECONDITION (`ggml_cuda_fattn_mma_kv_native_supported` -- 'Otherwise it converts the whole cache to f16 on every call, so keep the vector kernel'); the measure-only switches removed in-PR; OUR BOX IS ADA, the league model's GQA is in the widened band; B 104->105 `one-warp-ordered-reduce-row-batched-no-smem-barrier` -- PrismML `483bdab` #368: the Pascal PQ2_0 mat-vec re-shapes 4 warps + smem + block barrier to ONE warp doing whole rows R=4 batched, 'bit-identical to the generic mmvq there', LICENSED BY REDUCTION-ORDER PRESERVATION (the chunk assignment lane + 32*w + 128*i and the ((v0+v1)+v2)+v3 fold + butterfly are the generic's own); secondary contents: the decode identity sum(q*(c-1)) = sum(q*c) - sum(q), the clamped-compute-discard tail, 2-byte u16 composition, the arch-BAND admission (>=PASCAL <VOLTA, ne11 <= 4); DISCLOSED: the identity/tail/alignment atoms are CONSUMED by B180/B217 (entries_gpu_49, the fork's own #306 Ada twin) -- the novel atom is the warp-topology deletion under the order-preservation license). ONE own rule: D `fused-read-write-dst-input-overlap-refusal` (#30795 — the `moe_reduce` fusion admission's missing dst-input overlap guard: the graph allocator computes tensor liveness on the UNFUSED graph, where the inputs are fully consumed by earlier nodes of the fused group, so it can legally recycle input memory as dst — in the fused kernel that recycling is a threadgroup-vs-threadgroup race producing rare non-deterministic wrong results (observed with test-llama-archs -a kolibri); the fix is the 5-line refusal 'Reject the fusion when dst overlaps either input, same guard as the mul_mat_add fusion. The unfused MUL + ADD path is always correct.'; the four decision clauses: liveness-authority split (per-node legality vs whole-group read/write set), the race-granularity boundary (elementwise same-thread read-then-write tolerates the alias — the deliberate in-place family — a REDUCING fusion does not), refusal = safe fall-through (the B176 superset law's cousin), and the guard set is PER-FUSION never family-inherited (the older mul_mat_add fusion carried the guard, this later one omitted it — the sibling-transcription class one level up).
